# Published Theorems, Trivial Stubs, and Junk Declarations Audit

> **Target:** Complete catalogue of all `387` `GlobalTheory.Published.*` definitions, `5` inductives, `131` bundle `Prop`s (`UnitaryGroupThirdPlaceHypotheses`, `ThetaLocalInputs`), `9` `UnusedSlot*` namespaces, trivial `Prop` stubs, and `57` live `*junk*` constants at `40e56bab`; companion JSON [published_catalogue.json](../outputs/decomp/published_catalogue.json) (`510` nodes, `228` edges, `6` claims) `[L]`.
> **Kernel check:** `0` `sorryAx`, standard axioms `[propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound]` (`kernel.db` `ALL` `tree_id=0`); compiler-generated `*.match_*`/`*.casesOn`/`*_proof_*` bodies stay `OPAQUE` (see [Scope & Tag Legend](README.md)).

---

## Executive Summary & Key Findings `[L]`

Across `1,338` live `GlobalTheory.Published.*` kernel declarations (`387` `def`, `5` `inductive`, `946` `thm`), `2,523` live `UnusedSlot*` declarations, and `57` live `*junk*` declarations (`0` in `stmt_reach`), six structural facts govern specification fidelity `[L]`:

1. One literal `True` dummy exists in `Published.*`, leaving three predicates unproved standalone: [UnusedSlot72](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/ThetaCorrespondence/ThetaPairLifts/LocalTypeDictionary/DiscreteSpectrumResidualClassification.lean#L7) (`abbrev := True`, `fan_in = 9`) populates `UnitaryGroupThirdPlaceHypotheses.siegelEisensteinSimplePole` via [a trivial proof](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/ThetaCorrespondence/ThetaPairLifts/ThirdPlaceSeries/EveryCharacterOfLiteratureOfVWin.lean#L505) (`value_nodes = 3`). Three `Prop` definitions (`IsAdmissible`, `HasMinimalDiscriminant`, `LogLine.IsHorizontalOn`) are unproved parameterized predicates instantiated inside theorems. All remaining `296` `Prop` definitions reach `deep_k2_max_vn >= 554` once `edges.kind = 2` delegation wrappers are expanded.
2. A hidden `∧ True ∧` conjunction slot inside `DoublingMethodLocalZetaIntegrals`: [DoublingMethodLocalZetaIntegrals](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/ThetaCorrespondence/ThetaPairLifts/LocalTypeDictionary/DiscreteSpectrumResidualClassification.lean#L6) aliases [ResidueKilled.DM](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/WeilBound/RepresentationTheoryGL/EisensteinResidues/AnisotropicGlobalForms/DmOfHyp.lean#L13) (`proof_value_nodes = 12`), whose theorem [dm_of_two](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/WeilBound/RepresentationTheoryGL/EisensteinResidues/AnisotropicGlobalForms/DmOfHyp.lean#L24) (`value_nodes = 2,081`) discharges a literal middle `∧ True ∧` conjunct with `trivial`.
3. Narrow CM restrictions and conditional local proofs: [BaseChangeDefiniteUnitaryGroup](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/ThetaCorrespondence/ThetaPairLifts/LocalTypeDictionary/DiscreteSpectrumResidualClassification.lean#L9) (`proof_value_nodes = 7`) and [BaseChangeIsobaricTransfer](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/ThetaCorrespondence/ThetaPairLifts/LocalTypeDictionary/DiscreteSpectrumResidualClassification.lean#L8) (`proof_value_nodes = 3`) alias `Narrow.lean` definitions restricted to quadratic CM extensions `E/E+` unramified at every finite prime (`IsUnramifiedAt`), while [TameLocal](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/PublishedTheorems/GaloisCohomology₂/ContinuousCycMod.lean#L155) only has conditional theorem [TameLocal_of_duality](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/PublishedTheorems/GaloisCohomology₂/ExistsCoprimePowOfMemIneKer.lean#L1076) (`value_nodes = 13`), assuming `LocalTateDuality` (proved separately).
4. The `131` distinct `Prop`s powering `UnitaryGroupThirdPlaceHypotheses` (`60` fields) and `ThetaLocalInputs` (`53` fields) resolve to `105` `Published.*` leaves, `20` non-`Published` leaves (`16` unconditionally proved including `RecTExists`, `3` conditional on `Row` in `CarUAlt`/`CuspUAlt`/`PairUAlt`, `1` completely unproved open hypothesis [UpperTheory.One.M.Row](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/Foundations/UnitarySphericalFixing/SatakeParameters/RecT.lean#L6)), and `6` nested `Prop` structures.
5. Two of nine `UnusedSlot*` namespaces are pure `:= True` stubs while four hold real mathematics: [UnusedSlot55](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/Foundations/GaloisRepresentations/TypeConditions/TrivialLocalCondition.lean#L89) (`fan_in = 3`, consumed in [CuspidalGaloisUnramified'](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/ThetaCorrespondence/ThetaPairLifts/ThirdPlaceSeries/CuspidalGaloisUnramified.lean#L33), `fan_in = 30`) and `UnusedSlot72` are pure `:= True`; [UnusedSlot39](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/BlockTheory/TwistedDiscreteSeries/HeckeValueIntegrality.lean#L36) mixes real Hecke integrality with live `trivialClassCondition := fun _ _ _ => True` (`fan_in = 7`); `UnusedSlot33`/`34`/`35`/`80` hold real mathematics (`2,501` live declarations combined); `UnusedSlot36` and `UnusedSlot38` have `0` live declarations.
6. Live `*junk*` witnesses execute unconditionally inside `StageU` while all other `34` are guarded or misnamed theorems: `23` declarations in [cfJunk](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/LatticeGeometry/InhabLoc.lean#L13) and [cxJunk / divXFl / mdlJunk](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Formalism/LocalToGlobal/LatticeGeometry/Inhabited.lean#L88) populate degenerate geometric fields of `DivX` inside `StageU` (`geoOver_stages`), prevented from unsoundness only because [anyGeometry](https://github.com/leanprover-community/riemann-hypothesis/blob/40e56bab026552e089d7afcfeff08d2defb9c005/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/Definitions/AutomorphicMembership/OverIx.lean#L19) (`fun _ _ _ => True`, `fan_in = 14`) drops all `Geo` side conditions.

### 1.2 Live Vacuous/Dropped Stubs vs. Universal Filters vs. Dead Stubs

Grep hits on `:= True`, `:= False`, `:= PUnit`, `fun _ => True`, and `∧ True` fall into four disjoint categories. Only the first two (`live-vacuous-stub`, `live-conj-true-slot`, `live-unit-model`, `live-dropped-slot`) drop assumptions on live proof paths; `live-universal-filter` predicates (`fun _ => True`) strengthen quantifiers (`∀`) or set upper-bound supersets (`⊆`) `[L]`.

## 3. Spotlight on Tiny `Published.*` Proofs (`proof_value_nodes < 500`) `[L]`

Counting directly on the matched theorem declaration (`proof_value_nodes < 500`) flags `171` of the `300` `GlobalTheory.Published.*` `Prop` definitions (`168` proved theorems plus `3` standalone-unproved predicates). Expanding `EveryCharacterOfLiteratureOfVWin` thin wrappers leaves `101` below `500`, and transitively walking `edges.kind = 2` (value dependencies excluding type-shared binders) over project declarations drops the count with `deep_k2_max_vn < 500` to **`4`**, zero of which are non-vacuous proved theorems `[L]`:

1. Pure `:= True` dummy (`1`): `UnusedSlot72` (`proof_value_nodes = 3`, `deep_k2_max_vn = 3` via `trivial`).
2. Parameterized predicates lacking standalone provers (`3`): `GrothendieckMessing.IsAdmissible`, `HasMinimalDiscriminant`, `LogLine.IsHorizontalOn` (`proof_value_nodes = 0`).
3. Hidden trivial conjunction and conditional tiny wrappers (`2` notable cases): `DoublingMethodLocalZetaIntegrals` (`proof_value_nodes = 12` → `dm_of_two` `vn = 2,081` discharges a literal `∧ True ∧`), and `TameLocal` (`TameLocal_of_duality`, `proof_value_nodes = 13`, assumes `LocalTateDuality`).
4. Thin wrappers over narrow specialization (`2` critical aliases): `BaseChangeDefiniteUnitaryGroup` (`proof_value_nodes = 7`) and `BaseChangeIsobaricTransfer` (`proof_value_nodes = 3`) expand (`deep_k2_max_vn = 830,336`) into `PositivityTheory.Rg.BcB.F.BC.limAtOf_holds`, restricted to CM extensions `E/E+` unramified at all finite places.

| `abstract-record` | 107 | 42 | Algebraic data/map/subgroup definitions (`65`), parameterized `Prop` predicates, or theorems quantified over abstract axiomatic interfaces (`LocPrime`/`Core l`). |
| `wrapper/alias` | 46 | 24 | `def_vn = 1` alias `Prop`s (`4`), pure conjunction bundlers (`20`), or Lean compiler `.match_*`/`.casesOn`/`inst*` auxiliaries (`22`). |
| `true-dummy` | 1 | 1 | Literal `:= True` stub (`UnusedSlot72`). |
